"It is entirely within the official rules of the Rating Game, you ignorant low-class," Ravel scoffed. "A team is permitted to bring a maximum of two vials. Considering your master possesses a dedicated, living healer in the form of Asia Argento, the tactical advantage was actually yours. You simply failed to utilize it effectively."

The psychological blow was heavy, but the physical reality of the board was about to become infinitely worse.

Yubelluna didn't launch an explosive barrage at Rias. She didn't engage Issei. Instead, she pointed her staff directly at Asia, unleashing a highly specialized, localized arcane binding spell. The magic wrapped around the young nun, completely sealing her ability to access her Sacred Gear.

Yubelluna then stepped back, bowing respectfully to her master. Her job was done. She had neutralized the Gremory healing factor.

Riser Phenex stepped forward, his eyes burning with the cold, arrogant fury he had been harboring since his humiliation in the clubroom. He unfurled his massive wings of fire.

He fought Rias and Issei completely alone.

It wasn't a battle. It was a systematic, agonizing slaughter. Riser bypassed Rias’s destructive demonic bursts with his sheer physical speed and regenerative capabilities, forcing Issei to take the brunt of his flaming assaults.

Issei was beaten, burned, and broken. He was smashed against the concrete, his ribs fracturing under Riser’s heavy boots. Yet, defying all biological logic, Issei kept standing back up. His eyes were glossed over, his consciousness completely faded, but his body continued to fight entirely on instinct, driven by his deeply ingrained desire to protect his master.

In the VIP box, Ren watched the brutal display. It wasn't the cinematic, awe-inspiring perseverance of Rock Lee standing up to Gaara with shattered limbs. It lacked that martial grace. This was a raw, pathetic, horrifyingly human struggle—a boy whose body refused to accept the reality of his own death, twitching and swinging wildly at shadows while a pureblood Devil slowly beat him into a bloody pulp.

The sheer, agonizing desperation of the scene finally broke the King’s resolve.

Unable to watch her Pawn be tortured to death for the sake of her pride, Rias sprinted across the roof, throwing her arms around Issei’s battered, unconscious body to shield him from Riser’s final, killing blow.

With tears streaming down her face, Rias looked up at the sky and shattered her own future.

"I resign!" Rias cried out, her voice echoing across the dimension. "The House of Gremory formally surrenders!"

### Section 3: The Price of Power and the Empty Room

#### Sub Section 1: Departures

The word "resign" echoed through the VIP viewing gallery, hanging heavily in the air. On the crystalline screens, the pocket dimension dissolved, showing Rias clutching Issei’s broken body as the medical teams rushed the field.

Sirzechs Lucifer let out a quiet, measured sigh, his crimson eyes reflecting a complex mixture of familial sorrow and calculating foresight. "It is over," the Crimson Satan stated softly. He turned away from the glass. "It is time to proceed to the next phase of the plan."

Grayfia materialized silently beside him, holding a silver datapad. "The medical teams have successfully secured the wounded, Lord Sirzechs. Lady Rias and her peerage are being stabilized."

Ren stood up from his velvet chair, casually brushing a speck of dust from his coat. "If the exhibition is concluded, then it is time for my departure as well."

Grayfia turned to him, her posture immaculate. "I can teleport you back to your residence in Kuoh, Ren."

"That will be unnecessary, but I appreciate the offer," Ren replied. He turned and offered a deep, perfectly executed bow of respect to the assembled nobility. "Lord Sirzechs, Lord Gremory, Lord Phenex, Grayfia. Thank you for the hospitality and the insightful conversation. It was a genuine honor to spectate alongside you all."

"The pleasure was ours, Ren," Sirzechs smiled warmly. "I look forward to our next meeting."

Ren straightened up. Instead of walking toward the door, he simply closed his eyes and synchronized his three Dantians. The VIP box, fortified by the highest tier of Devil defensive magic, suddenly shuddered. The temperature plummeted to absolute zero as Ren imposed his will upon the fabric of reality.

With a sickening tear, the air in the center of the luxurious room ripped open.

A portal to the Harrowing Interstice materialized, bleeding pitch-black mist and the terrifying scent of ozone, ash, and multiversal decay. The distant, agonizing wails of forgotten entities echoed into the VIP box. Without a second glance, Ren stepped through the violent, violet tear. The portal snapped shut behind him, leaving no trace he had ever been there.

A profound, stunned silence fell over the gallery.

Lord Gremory stared at the empty space, his aristocratic composure entirely shattered. Lord Phenex swallowed hard. Even Sirzechs and Grayfia, who had thoroughly enjoyed the boy's polite and intellectual conversation, felt a cold knot form in their stomachs. The polite, respectful human scholar had just casually ripped a hole into a dimension of pure, concentrated nightmare and walked into it like he was taking a stroll through a park.

While Ren plunged into the abyss, Issei plunged into his own subconscious.

He was floating in a dark, empty void. It wasn't the first time he had been here. After the church raid, during a particularly vivid, degenerate dream about finally sleeping with Rias, he had heard a deep, ancient sigh echo through his mind. A voice had spoken from the darkness: *“Finally. You are strong enough to hear my voice.”*

Now, the voice returned. But this time, the darkness parted.

Two massive, glowing green eyes ignited in the void. A gargantuan red dragon, exuding an aura of primordial, world-ending power, materialized before him. Ddraig, the Welsh Dragon, the Heavenly Dragon of Domination.

*“You have grown much stronger, partner,”* Ddraig rumbled, his voice shaking the very foundations of Issei’s soul. *“Yet, I feel your despair. The agonizing regret of losing your master. The frustration of your own weakness.”*

"I couldn't protect her," Issei wept, floating in the dark. "I wasn't strong enough. Riser took her, and I couldn't do a damn thing!"

*“Do you desire the power to change this reality?”* Ddraig asked, leaning his massive, scaled snout closer. *“The power to shatter the Balance? I can force an incomplete awakening of your Scale Mail. I can give you ten seconds of absolute, transcendent power. Enough to crush that burning bird.”*

"Give it to me!" Issei begged desperately. "I'll do anything!"

*“Power of this magnitude cannot simply be given, Issei. It requires a sacrifice of equal weight,”* Ddraig warned, his green eyes narrowing. *“To forge the armor, I require a piece of your humanity. Give me your left arm. Permanently.”*

Issei didn't even hesitate. The image of Rias crying as she surrendered flashed in his mind.

"Take it!" Issei roared, thrusting his left arm forward. "Take my arm, take whatever you need! Just give me the power to save the President!"

The dragon roared in approval, and a blinding crimson light engulfed the void.

Issei’s eyes snapped open. He gasped, sitting bolt upright in his bed in the Kuoh Academy infirmary. His body screamed in agony from Riser's beating, but his left arm felt incredibly strange, thrumming with a dormant, terrifying heat.

"You are awake," a calm voice noted.

Issei turned his head. Grayfia Lucifuge was standing beside his bed.

"Where is Rias?!" Issei demanded, scrambling to get up.

"She has returned to the Underworld. The engagement party is being held tonight," Grayfia explained smoothly. She reached into her apron and placed a piece of parchment bearing a glowing, crimson teleportation seal on his bedside table. It bore the crest of a Griffin.

"Lord Sirzechs asked me to deliver this to you, along with a message," Grayfia said, her silver eyes locking onto Issei’s. "He said: *If you want to save my sister, then crash the party.*"

Issei gripped the seal, his heart pounding in his chest. Sirzechs was giving him a backdoor.

"I need backup," Issei muttered, ignoring the pain in his fractured ribs. He threw on his clothes and sprinted out of the infirmary, heading straight for Ren's apartment. If there was anyone who could help him assassinate Riser Phenex and ruin an aristocratic wedding, it was the human anomaly who had single-handedly broken Riser’s vanguard.

Issei reached the apartment complex and pounded on Ren’s door. No answer. He forced the lock, bursting inside.

The apartment was dark, cold, and entirely empty. The tactical gear was gone. The canvas duffel bags were gone. It looked as though nobody had lived there for months.

"Dammit, Ren, where did you go?!" Issei panicked. He grabbed a pen and scribbled a frantic, desperate message on a piece of paper, slamming it onto the kitchen counter. *Ren - Going to the Underworld to save Rias. Meet us at the ORC clubroom immediately. We need you! - Issei.*

Issei sprinted all the way back to the academy. When he burst into the Occult Research Club, the rest of the peerage—Akeno, Kiba, Koneko, and Asia—were already gathered, fully armed and radiating fierce determination.

"Issei," Akeno smiled, though her eyes were sharp. "Are you ready?"

"I left a message for Ren," Issei panted, clutching the Griffin seal. "We just need to wait for him. With his combat skills, we can easily bypass Riser's security."

They waited. The clock on the clubroom wall ticked mercilessly. Five minutes. Ten minutes. Fifteen minutes.

The silence in the room grew heavy. The engagement party was starting. They were running out of time.

"He isn't coming, Issei," Kiba said quietly, his hand resting on his sword hilt. "He is a human. He has no obligation to this House."

Issei stared at the door, his heart sinking, before his expression hardened into pure resolve. Ren had told him to forge himself into a weapon. Now, he had to prove he could be one without having his hand held.

"Fine," Issei growled, channeling his draconic aura into the Griffin seal. "We do this ourselves. Let's go save the President."

The crimson circle flared, and the Occult Research Club vanished from the human world.

#### Sub Section 2: Return to Basic

The crushing gravity of the Harrowing Interstice pressed against Ren’s physical form, an endless expanse of ashen wasteland beneath a sky bleeding with violet nebulas. For hours, he had sat in a perfect lotus position, cycling the agonizing, heavy dark energy of the void through his newly unblocked major chakras. It was a terrifying, infinite power source, but as Ren analyzed the flow of the cosmic current, his analytical mind caught a fundamental discrepancy. He was acting solely as a conduit for an external force. He was borrowing the universe's despair, but he had entirely neglected his own biological engine.

*What about my own internal energy?* Ren questioned silently into the roaring storms of the abyss. *What about my own Qi?*

The Harrowing Interstice did not answer with words, but with a profound, resonant vibration that shook the marrow of his bones. It was a sensation of ancient, cosmic acknowledgment. Slowly, the overwhelming pressure of the void’s multiversal despair—the screaming wails, the horrifying visions of shattered empires—receded. The dark energy did not vanish; it simply took a respectful step back, creating an absolute, frictionless vacuum within his meridians, inviting him to ignite his own core.

Ren focused inward, diving past the external darkness to the very center of his being. He commanded his three synchronized Dantians to generate their own internal kinetic force.

A new energy flared to life inside him.

It was a manifestation of pure, unadulterated Qi. True to his nature, its affinity was absolute darkness—a deep, consuming blackness that mirrored the void. Yet, unlike the energy of the Interstice, this internal power was completely silent. There were no weeping souls, no echoes of multiversal tragedy, and no chaotic malice. It was pristine, obedient, and entirely his own. Ren exhaled a neuro-somatic breath, seamlessly weaving his own pitch-black Qi with the infinitely dense, chaotic energy of the Harrowing Interstice. He embraced both: the perfect, silent engine of his human soul, and the limitless, roaring fuel of the void. He had achieved true, terrifying equilibrium.

Sensing the stabilization of his power, Ren forced the fabric of the dimension to rupture, stepping back into the quiet darkness of his Kuoh apartment.

The moment his boots touched the floorboards, he saw the frantic, scribbled note illuminated by the streetlights streaming through his window. *Going to the Underworld to save Rias. Meet us at the ORC clubroom immediately. We need you! - Issei.*

Ren checked the digital clock on his nightstand. It had been hours since the Rating Game concluded. The Occult Research Club was long gone, and the aristocratic engagement party in the Underworld was undoubtedly already underway. Standard transportation magic was out of the question; the Devil King’s security grid would incinerate any unauthorized teleportation circles.

"Inefficient," Ren murmured, tossing the note aside.

He didn't need Devil magic. He closed his eyes, tapping into the newly harmonized, pitch-black Qi circulating through his spine. He reached through the veil of the human world, grasped the spatial coordinates of the Underworld through the sheer density of its demonic pressure, and ripped a jagged shortcut straight through the Harrowing Interstice.

In the heart of the Underworld, the grand, opulent ballroom of the Phenex estate was in utter chaos.

Issei, Kiba, Koneko, and Asia had just violently breached the heavy mahogany doors, weapons drawn and demonic auras flaring. High-class nobles gasped in aristocratic outrage. Riser Phenex, dressed in an immaculate white tuxedo, glared down from the grand staircase, his eyes burning with lethal fury at the sight of the low-class Devils ruining his victory celebration.

Before Riser could order his royal guards to execute the intruders, the temperature in the center of the grand ballroom plummeted to absolute zero.

A terrifying, violent tearing sound drowned out the orchestral music. Between the furious Gremory peerage and the shocked Devil nobility, reality simply shattered. A jagged, violet rift bled into the pristine ballroom, exuding a suffocating, hostile atmospheric pressure that forced several high-class Devils to their knees in sheer panic.

Ren stepped out of the void, his dark coat billowing against the dimensional winds. The tear snapped shut behind him, leaving a deathly, horrified silence in its wake. The nobles stared at the human anomaly, their magical senses screaming in confusion at the absolute lack of mana and the terrifying density of his presence.

Issei’s face lit up with overwhelming relief. "Ren! You made it! Let’s tear this place apart and get the President back!"

Ren did not summon his dense gravitational pressure. He did not drop into a martial stance. Instead, his dark eyes casually scanned the perimeter of the grand ballroom until he spotted the seventy-foot-long, lavishly decorated aristocratic buffet table.

Without a word, Ren walked right past a bewildered squad of Phenex royal guards, picked up a pristine porcelain plate, and began meticulously loading it with high-end Underworld delicacies. He selected a generous portion of roasted griffin flank, a cluster of glowing spectral grapes, and poured himself a glass of vintage elven wine.

Finding a plush velvet chair near the ice sculpture, Ren sat down, crossed his legs, and took a bite of the roast, chewing thoughtfully.

Issei’s jaw practically unhinged. Kiba nearly dropped his demonic sword.

"What the hell are you doing?!" Issei shrieked, sprinting over to the buffet table. "We are in the middle of a suicide mission to rescue Rias! Why are you eating the hors d'oeuvres?!"

Ren took a sip of his wine, looking at Issei with cold, absolute calm. "Because the griffin flank is exceptionally well-seasoned, Issei. Furthermore, you do not need my assistance to rescue the President. We are not crashing a party; we are performing on a stage."

"What are you talking about?!" Issei demanded, waving his red, draconically-empowered left arm in the air.

"I was sitting directly next to Lord Sirzechs in the VIP gallery during your Rating Game," Ren answered smoothly, cutting another piece of meat. "I already read the script for tonight’s performance. We are all fully authorized, invited guests. So, lower your voice and enjoy the catering before the main event begins."

"The human is entirely correct," a booming, charismatic voice echoed through the grand hall.

The crowd of panicked nobles instantly parted, bowing deeply in absolute reverence as Sirzechs Lucifer, the Crimson Satan, strode down the grand staircase. He ignored the furious sputtering of Lord Phenex and the enraged glare of Riser, walking directly toward the center of the ballroom with a theatrical smile.

"A wedding ceremony following a one-sided surrender is horribly dull, don't you all agree?" Sirzechs projected his voice to the gallery of nobles, weaving a masterful web of political manipulation and aristocratic pride. "The Underworld is a realm built on power and spectacle. I promised you all a show tonight, and what better entertainment could there possibly be than a mythic clash of titans?"

Sirzechs turned his piercing crimson eyes toward Riser. "Riser Phenex. You claim to be the immortal bird, a pureblood noble worthy of inheriting the Gremory legacy. If your absolute superiority is genuine, then a low-class, fledgling dragon should pose absolutely no threat to you. I propose a final, public duel. The Dragon against the Phoenix."

Riser gritted his teeth, the sheer pressure of the Crimson Satan's taunt forcing his hand. To refuse a duel from a low-class Devil in front of the entire Underworld aristocracy would permanently shatter the Phenex family's pride.

Sirzechs then turned to Issei, his smile widening as he felt the terrifying, dormant heat radiating from the boy's sacrificed left arm. "And for you, Red Dragon Emperor. If you can achieve the impossible and defeat an immortal noble, the Underworld will grant you whatever your heart desires. Do you want ultimate political power? Infinite, unfathomable wealth? Or perhaps a harem of the most beautiful succubi in existence?"

Ren paused his meal, watching Issei over the rim of his wine glass. He knew exactly what the Pawn would say.

Issei didn't hesitate. He didn't look at the gold, the nobles, or the promise of infinite women. He raised his draconic gauntlet, pointing it directly at Riser Phenex.

"I don't care about the wealth, and I don't care about the power!" Issei roared, his voice echoing with the primal, world-ending fury of the Welsh Dragon. "I just want my President back!"

### Section 4: The Dragon and the Phoenix

The grand ballroom of the Phenex estate violently transformed into a coliseum of scorched marble and shattered crystal. Riser Phenex, wreathed in the immortal flames of his lineage, stared down at the low-class Pawn who dared to challenge him. But this time, Issei was not a broken, bleeding victim. A blinding crimson light erupted from his sacrificed left arm, instantly encasing his entire body in the jagged, terrifyingly heavy draconic plating of the incomplete Scale Mail.

The sheer density of the aura radiating from the armor forced the surrounding high-class nobles to erect hasty defensive barriers.

Riser’s eyes widened, a genuine spark of warrior’s respect flashing through his aristocratic arrogance. He unleashed a massive torrent of hellfire, but Issei stepped right through the inferno, the draconic armor absorbing the heat entirely.

"Incredible," Riser murmured, parrying a devastating right hook from Issei that cracked the floorboards beneath them. "To unlock the armor of the Heavenly Dragon through sheer force of will... you are a genuine anomaly, Hyoudou Issei. When Rias and I are married, I will take you into my own peerage. I will personally guide you. I will forge you into the greatest warrior the Underworld has ever seen."

"I don't want your guidance, and I don't give a damn about your peerage!" Issei roared, his voice metallic and deep through the dragon helm. He swung a heavy, mana-infused kick that sent Riser skidding across the room. "I am here to save Rias!"

Riser recovered instantly, his wounds sealing in a burst of healing fire. His expression hardened into grim, absolute duty. "You are acting like a spoiled child playing hero! Look around you! Look at the empty seats of the Great King Houses!" Riser summoned a massive halberd made of pure, concentrated flame. "The Great War nearly drove Devil-kind to extinction! This marriage is not a whim; it is a biological and political necessity. The Gremory and Phenex lines must merge to secure the future of the Underworld! You are trying to destroy our entire society for a selfish crush!"

The two clashed again, a shockwave of fire and draconic power shattering the grand chandelier above.

"You think I care about the politics of a world I didn't even know existed a month ago?!" Issei screamed, forcefully breaking Riser's guard and burying a gauntleted fist directly into the pureblood's chest. "I saw her face, Riser! When you were beating me to death on that roof, I saw Rias throw away all her pride, her dreams, and her freedom just to stop my pain! I saw her cry!"

From the sidelines, Rias gasped, her hands flying to her mouth as tears welled in her eyes once more.

"There is absolutely nothing in this universe more important to me than fighting for her happiness!" Issei roared, channeling every ounce of his soul into his engines. "I will shatter your society if it means making her smile!"

The sheer, raw conviction in Issei's voice resonated through the entire ballroom. But passion alone could not sustain the impossible. The ten-second timer of the forcefully awakened Scale Mail struck zero.

With the sound of shattering glass, the crimson armor violently fractured and dissolved, leaving Issei battered and exposed in his school uniform.

Riser exhaled a breath of fire, raising his flaming halberd for the executioner's blow. "Your time is up, Dragon. You fought beautifully, but a Devil cannot outlast an immortal."

"Who said I was fighting like a Devil?" Issei grinned, his left arm—now permanently scaled and glowing with raw, untamed draconic heat—reaching into his pocket.

Because his left arm had been sacrificed to Ddraig, it was no longer bound by the demonic biology of a Devil. It was the limb of a Heavenly Dragon. Issei pulled out a heavy silver cross and a crystal vial of highly concentrated, church-blessed holy water. The surrounding Devils gasped in absolute horror at the presence of the lethal artifacts.

"Boost!" the gauntlet announced. Issei shattered the vial over the silver cross, gripping it tightly.

Instead of burning him to ash, Issei channeled the *Boosted Gift* directly into the holy artifacts. The ambient holy energy, lethal to any creature of the Underworld, was multiplied exponentially. A blinding, pure white light erupted from the cross.

"This is for the President!" Issei yelled, lunging forward with a speed Riser could not track.

He slammed the drastically overpowered holy cross directly into Riser's chest. The holy energy violently bypassed the Phenex regeneration, entirely neutralizing the immortal flames. Riser let out a breathless gasp of shock as the holy explosion engulfed him, launching him across the ballroom and crashing into the far wall, completely unconscious.

The Underworld fell deathly silent. A low-class Pawn had just defeated a pureblood heir.

Breathing heavily, Issei ignored the stunned aristocracy and limped directly toward Rias. He reached into his jacket and pulled out the teleportation scroll Grayfia had secretly given him. He crushed it in his hand. A massive, glowing magic circle expanded across the shattered floor, and with a deafening screech, a majestic, fully armored Griffon materialized in the center of the ballroom.

Issei hopped onto the beast, extending his hand to Rias. She took it without hesitation, pulling herself up behind him and wrapping her arms tightly around his waist.

"Lord Gremory, Lord Phenex," Issei announced, looking up at the shocked patriarchs on the balcony. "We are leaving!"

Before anyone could object, the Griffon flapped its massive wings, shattering the grand stained-glass windows and launching into the crimson sky of the Underworld.

High above the chaotic estate, the cold night air rushed past them. Rias leaned against Issei’s back, the overwhelming relief washing away the nightmare of the past ten days. She gently urged Issei to turn around. When he did, she pulled him in by the collar and kissed him deeply under the glow of the purple moon.

It was a profound, paradigm-shifting moment. As they broke apart, the aristocratic mask was gone. She was no longer looking at him as her loyal servant or her disposable Pawn. She was looking at a man who had ripped a hole in reality to save her.

"Issei," Rias whispered, a playful, incredibly genuine smile dancing on her lips. "If you wish, tonight... I could offer you my virginity."

"Yes!" Issei answered instantly, his draconic aura flaring in pure, unadulterated excitement.

Rias burst into genuine, musical laughter. His complete lack of tact, his utter honesty, and his ridiculous perversion were the most comforting things in the world to her at that moment. He hadn't changed; he was just uniquely, fiercely hers.

Back in the ruined ballroom, the silence broke. Ravel Phenex sprinted across the rubble, tears streaming down her face as she reached her unconscious brother. Riser slowly opened his eyes, groaning as the lingering holy damage stung his chest.

"Lord Brother, please, let me help you!" Ravel pleaded, reaching for him.

Riser gently raised a hand, stopping her. His arrogant fire was entirely gone, replaced by a quiet, hollow realization. "It's okay, Ravel. Let me be." He slowly pulled himself up, turning his back on the crowd, and walked quietly out of the hall alone, the crushing weight of his first true defeat sealing the doors of his pride.

Up on the balcony, Lord Phenex watched his son walk away. Slowly, a warm, relieved smile spread across his face. He turned to Sirzechs and Lord Gremory.

"Thank you, both of you," Lord Phenex said softly. "This is exactly what Riser desperately needed. He has never known the taste of dirt, and his arrogance was poisoning his potential. This defeat will finally teach him what it means to grow."

Lord Gremory nodded, swirling a glass of wine. "It seems we have all learned a lesson tonight. Our intelligence ministries have finalized their demographic reassessments. The Devil population has stabilized far better than our post-war estimates projected. The era of panic is over." Zeoticus looked out the shattered window, tracking the distant silhouette of the Griffon. "We no longer need to force our children into loveless, desperate engagements just to ensure the survival of purebloods. I must admit... waking up from that nightmare of fear, and seeing my daughter truly free, is the greatest relief a father could ask for."

Down on the main floor, the Occult Research Club was packing up. Akeno, Kiba, Koneko, and Asia stood near Grayfia, ready to return to the human world.

Ren walked up to the group, casually wiping his mouth with a linen napkin, having successfully finished his plate of Underworld delicacies during the chaos of Issei’s victory.

"Ren?" Akeno blinked. "I thought you would have ripped open another terrifying dimension to go home by now."

"Standard teleportation is significantly less taxing on the metaphysical state of my soul after a heavy meal," Ren replied calmly, taking his place in the center of Grayfia's transportation circle. "Furthermore, I need you all to deliver a message."

"To Issei?" Kiba asked, sheathing his sword.

"Correct. I am riding with you because I refuse to be the third wheel interrupting a profoundly overdue romantic flight," Ren stated, folding his arms as the silver magic circle began to glow. "When lover boy finally returns to Kuoh, inform him that there is something of absolute, critical importance I must speak with him about."

## Life Ending 
### Section 1: Farewell Matsuda and Motohama

The weekend morning air in Kuoh was crisp and deceptively normal, completely betraying the multiversal upheaval that had occurred just days prior. Ren sat by the window of his sparsely furnished apartment, meditating on the silent, pitch-black Qi circulating through his Dantians, when a rapid, energetic knocking shattered the quiet.

Issei stood in the hallway, practically vibrating with a chaotic mixture of leftover draconic adrenaline and sheer teenage euphoria. He was no longer the desperate, beaten Pawn. He carried himself with the subtle but undeniable weight of a dragon who had successfully hoarded his treasure.

"Akeno passed on your message," Issei said, leaning against the doorframe with a wide grin. "Said it was super important. But honestly, man, my schedule is completely booked today. The President—well, Rias—is throwing a massive celebration party at the Occult Research Club this evening to officially welcome me back and celebrate the broken engagement. You have to come. We can talk about whatever it is there, over a mountain of high-end Underworld steak."

Ren studied the boy for a moment, noting the relaxed set of his shoulders and the lingering spark of raw affection in his eyes when he said Rias's name. The urgency of Ren's impending departure could wait a few more hours.

"Very well," Ren agreed calmly, stepping back to close the door. "Enjoy your preparations, Issei. I will see you at the clubroom tonight."

With the evening secured, Ren turned his attention to the loose ends he had left tethered to the mundane world. If he was truly going to step fully into the abyss to forge his own reality, he owed his former comrades a proper severance.

An hour later, Ren was standing in the neon-drenched cacophony of the local arcade, handing a massive stack of tokens to Matsuda and Motohama.

"I am funding this entirely," Ren announced, his tone flat but his eyes carrying a rare, genuine amusement. "Whatever games you wish to play, whatever food you wish to consume, consider it handled."

The remaining half of Kuoh Academy's infamous perverted quartet did not need to be told twice. What followed was a marathon of absolute, unregulated teenage indulgence. Ren watched with quiet analytical detachment as Matsuda violently mashed buttons on a fighting game cabinet, while Motohama calculated absolute frame data and still managed to lose. They moved from the arcade to a karaoke parlor, where Ren sat on a leather sofa, sipping cold green tea while the two boys butchered pop anthems with tone-deaf enthusiasm.

As the sun began to set, casting long orange shadows over the city, Ren guided them to a bustling, smoke-filled all-you-can-drink barbecue restaurant. The grills sizzled with cheap, fatty cuts of pork, and the table was quickly littered with empty soda and low-tier beer glasses.

The alcohol and heavy food eventually unraveled their inhibitions, bringing the underlying tension to the surface.

"It’s just not fair, man," Matsuda slurred slightly, aggressively flipping a piece of meat on the grill. "First Issei gets scouted by the most beautiful girls in the entire school. Then you start hanging out with them too! You two totally abandoned us for the Occult Research Club!"

"Statistically speaking, the probability of both of you infiltrating that social circle while we remain ostracized is completely absurd," Motohama added, pushing his glasses up his nose with a pout. "We know you've been going to their clubroom, Ren. Are you holding out on us? Is Issei actually living the dream?"

Ren rested his chin on his hand, letting out a soft, genuine laugh. It was a rare sound, devoid of his usual tactical coldness. He looked at the two normal boys—petty, perverted, but fiercely loyal to their own mundane desires. He was about to leave this world behind forever. He could easily tell them he was transferring schools or moving overseas, wrapping his departure in a comfortable, easily digestible lie.

But looking at his oldest friends, Ren decided that a polite lie was an insult to their shared history.

"I am leaving Kuoh," Ren stated smoothly, his voice slicing through the noisy ambient chatter of the restaurant. "I will be going very far away, and I will not be returning."

Matsuda dropped his tongs. Motohama blinked, the alcohol haze instantly clearing from his eyes. "Wait, what? Like... moving? Where?"

"To answer your earlier question," Ren continued, ignoring the interruption and leaning forward. "Yes. Issei has completely fulfilled the dream. He is living in a reality that far exceeds your wildest, most degenerate imaginations. He is experiencing mixed baths, undivided loyalty, and the absolute affection of the most beautiful women in this city."

Matsuda’s face contorted into an expression of profound, agonizing jealousy, but Ren raised a single, commanding finger, freezing him in place.

"However," Ren’s voice dropped an octave, the temperature around their booth noticeably chilling. "The Occult Research Club is not what it appears to be. It is not a harem comedy. It is an active battlefield. The things lurking in Issei’s shadow would snap your minds just by looking at them."

Ren met their confused, terrified eyes with absolute sincerity. "If you truly desire to stand beside Issei and enter that club, you must completely forge yourselves into weapons. You must become strong enough to survive lethal encounters, and more importantly, strong enough to not become a liability that gets Issei killed protecting you. If you cannot do that, remain exactly where you are."

Matsuda and Motohama stared at him, their mouths hanging open in utter bewilderment.

"What... what the hell are you talking about, Ren?" Motohama stammered. "Battlefields? Weapons? You're not making any sense."

"I am providing the answer to your puzzle," Ren said simply. He stood up from the booth, dropping a thick stack of yen on the table to cover the exorbitant bill.

Rather than walking toward the exit, Ren turned toward the solid brick wall beside their table. He closed his eyes, synchronized his Dantians, and casually reached his hand forward.

Reality tore open like wet paper.

A jagged portal to the Harrowing Interstice violently manifested right inside the restaurant. The smell of grilled meat was instantly overwhelmed by the horrifying scent of ozone, ash, and multiversal decay. The distant, agonizing wails of forgotten entities bled through the violet rift, creating a suffocating pressure that made Matsuda and Motohama fall backward out of their chairs in sheer, unadulterated terror.

"Farewell, my friends," Ren said, glancing over his shoulder one last time.

Without another word, he stepped directly into the nightmare dimension. The portal snapped shut behind him, the brick wall sealing seamlessly, leaving Matsuda and Motohama trembling on the restaurant floor, desperately questioning their own sanity.

Minutes later, the violent tearing of space echoed in the woods behind Kuoh Academy. Ren stepped out of the Interstice, his dark coat settling as he approached the old school building.

The Occult Research Club was practically vibrating with life. Music pulsed through the thick wooden doors, and the scent of high-end Underworld cuisine wafted into the night air. Ren opened the door and stepped inside, instantly enveloped in the warmth of the celebration.

The room was draped in banners. Akeno was playfully feeding Issei a piece of steak, much to the Pawn's bright red embarrassment, while Rias watched them with a serene, genuinely happy smile. Kiba and Koneko were quietly enjoying the buffet, the heavy tension of the past two weeks completely erased from their shoulders.

"Ren! You made it!" Issei called out, gently pushing Akeno's hand away and jogging over.

"I told you I would," Ren replied, grabbing a glass of sparkling cider from a passing tray.

He integrated himself into the party seamlessly, observing the joyous interactions and partaking in the exorbitant feast. He watched Rias laugh freely, unburdened by the chains of her lineage, and he watched Issei bask in the glow of the family he had fought so brutally to protect. Ren allowed the warmth of the room to wash over him for a long, quiet hour, perfectly memorizing the peace he had helped orchestrate.

Eventually, the music lulled, and the group settled into a comfortable, deeply contented downtime. Ren set his empty glass on a mahogany side table. His dark eyes locked onto the Red Dragon Emperor.

"Issei," Ren said, his voice cutting through the relaxed atmosphere with a sharp, undeniable gravity. "Are you ready to talk?"

### Section 2: Farewell ORC

"Remember the portal I opened in the VIP gallery? The tear in the fabric of space?" Ren began, his dark eyes locking onto the Red Dragon Emperor, his tone devoid of its usual casual cadence. "It leads to a dimension called the Harrowing Interstice. I will be leaving Kuoh, Issei. I am going there."

Issei physically jolted, the joyous, relaxed posture he had carried all evening instantly vanishing. His draconic aura flared with sudden, erratic anxiety. "Wait, what?! Why? Did I do something wrong, man? Did we alienate you after the Rating Game? Because if this is about me hogging all the glory, or if you feel left out—"

"Issei, calm down," Ren interrupted, though his voice held a rare, reassuring warmth. "I had not finished my statement."

The sudden tension in Issei’s voice had acted like a magnet, drawing the attention of the entire clubroom. The music seemed to fade into the background as Akeno, Kiba, Koneko, Asia, and Rias all turned toward the two boys, the celebratory atmosphere giving way to a heavy, respectful silence.

Rias stepped forward, her crimson hair catching the ambient light of the room. She looked at Ren not as a high-class Devil looking down on a human, but as a commander assessing an equal.

"If you are leaving because you feel you have no place here, Ren, then you are mistaken," Rias said, her voice projecting the absolute authority and newfound maturity of the Gremory House. "I am formally inviting you to join the Occult Research Club. Not as a servant, and not as a reincarnated Devil bound to my pieces, but as a human member of our family."

She paused, offering him a soft, apologetic smile. "Previously, I maintained a strict barrier regarding humans. It was not out of arrogance, but out of fear. The enemies we face are lethal, and I was terrified of accepting members who could not survive the crossfire, or whose presence would force my peerage into fatal, defensive compromises. But you have proven, beyond a shadow of a doubt, that you are not a liability. You are a vanguard in your own right."

Ren looked at the crimson-haired president, a slow, genuine smile spreading across his face. "I know, Rias. And I appreciate the tactical logic behind your previous hesitations."

Ren turned his gaze back to Issei, and then swept his eyes over the rest of the peerage. "Rias likely extended this invitation tonight because she remembers the conversation we had after the church raid. When you were training, Issei, I pointed out the sheer, absurd disparity in our realities. We all shared the exact same degenerate, mundane dreams—Matsuda, Motohama, and myself. Yet, by some stroke of multiversal luck, you were the one who was revived. You were the one granted the 'protagonist's' harem paradise, while the rest of us were left to rot in the background of your story."

Issei looked down, a flash of guilt crossing his face, but Ren raised a hand.

"Do not misunderstand me," Ren continued, his voice echoing with philosophical weight. "I hold no jealousy toward your life, Issei. The difference between myself and the others is that my ambitions do not end at the boundaries of a harem. If the universe refuses to hand me a paradise, then I will carve my own path. I will pursue the absolute pinnacle of scholarship, martial perfection, and multiversal strength."

"You certainly have the terrifying intellect for it," Akeno chimed in, a fond, teasing smile on her lips, though her violet eyes held deep respect. "Though we will miss having such a ruthlessly pragmatic mind around to keep Issei in check."

"You are a true warrior, Ren," Kiba added, resting a hand on the hilt of his wooden practice sword. "You walk a different path than the sword, but your dedication to your craft is something I deeply admire. You have my respect, wherever you go."

Koneko took a slow bite of her chocolate crepe, looking directly at Ren with her usual deadpan expression, though the hostility that used to define their interactions was entirely gone. "You're less of a creep than I thought. Don't die out there."

"Please be careful, Ren-san," Asia said softly, clutching her hands together in prayer. "We will always pray for your safety."

Ren bowed his head slightly in acknowledgment of their words before turning back to Issei. "What I said after the church raid remains true, but my departure is not born from resentment. I am not leaving forever, nor am I leaving because I dislike Kuoh or the company in this room. I am going to the Harrowing Interstice because the nature of my Qi demands a crucible. It is a place I simply *must* go to forge my reality."

Ren looked at Rias, his dark eyes reflecting the warm light of the clubroom. "That being said, I do not require a Devil Piece, nor do I require a physical token. But I gladly accept your verbal invitation, President. I am officially a member of the Occult Research Club."

A collective breath of relief and joy washed over the room. Issei’s draconic aura settled into a warm, thrumming hum, and a massive grin broke across his face. "You hear that? You're stuck with us now, Ren! Which means you have to come back eventually so we can make you pay club dues!"

"I look forward to the invoice," Ren replied dryly.

He stepped back from the group, moving toward the center of the spacious clubroom. He closed his eyes, inhaling a deep, stabilizing breath. He synchronized his three Dantians, merging his silent, pitch-black human Qi with the latent memory of the void.

He raised his hand and sliced downward.

The fabric of reality within the Occult Research Club violently tore open. The warm, festive air of the room was instantly sucked toward the jagged, violet rift. The horrifying, sub-zero atmospheric pressure of the Harrowing Interstice bled through, carrying the faint, distant wails of forgotten entities and the scent of cosmic ash.

Rias and her peerage stood their ground, their demonic auras flaring instinctively against the oppressive, multiversal dread pouring from the portal. They watched in silent, profound awe as the human anomaly stood perfectly unaffected before the abyss.

Ren cast one final look over his shoulder, locking eyes with Issei, Rias, and the found family that had anchored his humanity. He offered them a sharp, respectful salute.

Without another word, Ren stepped through the tear. The dimensional rift snapped shut behind him with a deafening crack, instantly restoring the peace, warmth, and quiet of the Occult Research Club, leaving nothing behind but the lingering memory of a human who had chosen to conquer the dark.
